So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.600 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 10.133 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.403 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.600 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 10.133 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.403 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.533 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 13.333 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.809 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.533 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 13.333 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.809 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.275 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.882 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.400 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.275 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.882 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.400 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 2.644 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.465 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = -0.444 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 2.644 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.465 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = -0.444 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.289 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.076 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 2.711 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.289 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.076 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 2.711 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.822 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 8.443 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 11.511 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.822 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 8.443 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 11.511 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 20.796 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.267 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 16.000 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 20.796 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.267 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 16.000 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>.
